Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The genomics operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 409. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 525 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_409(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 409. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^525(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 211.5 (Spectral Property)
